Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : MHL08 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : BOMU-CS
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Çok seviyeli ürün aðacý - Kod bazýnda icmalli, Excel
Kod : 
Sub PrintForm()
	Dim ExcelSheet, xl
	Set ExcelSheet = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	ExcelSheet.Visible = False
	Set xl = ExcelSheet.Workbooks.Add

	Doc.CreateTableObject "ABC=WBMRSYS","TMPCOPY"

	Set ML_Table = Doc.GetTableObject("ABC")

	Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject("MULTILEVEL")
	ML_Table.AppendTable(ML)

	'ML_Table.Sort("BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE")
	'ML_Table.GroupBy("BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE+PICTURE_LEVEL2")

	xl.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Value = "Mamul Kodu"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(2,1).Value = "Mamul Miktarý"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(1,2).Value = BOMU01E.MAMULCODE
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(2,2).Value = BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR


	satir = 5
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,1).Value = "Seviye"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,2).Value = "Hammadde Kodu"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,3).Value = "Hammadde Adý"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,4).Value = "Miktar"

	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,6).Value = "ÜRETÝLEN / SATIN ALINAN"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,5).Value = "BÝRÝM"

	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,7).Value = "TEDARÝK EDÝLEN YAN SANAYÝ"

	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,9).Value = "BÝRÝM FÝYAT"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,10).Value = "BÝRÝM"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,11).Value = "KÝÞÝ / PARÇA ADET"


	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,12).Value = "ÇEVRÝM SÜRESÝ SN"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,13).Value = "MAKÝNA GRUBU3"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,14).Value = "MAKÝNA GRUBU2"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,15).Value = "Göz"
	xl.Sheets(1).cells(4,16).Value = "MAKÝNA GRUBU2"


ReDim UrunKod(10)

	For i = 1 To ML_Table.GetRecCount()
			KAYNAK= (trim(ML_Table.PICTURE_LEVEL2(i))) 
			xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,17).Value =KAYNAK

			seviye=Trim(ML_Table.XLEVEL(i))

			StokKodu = trim(ML_Table.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i))
			Miktar = ML_Table.KAYNAKMIKTART(i)

			IF KAYNAK="AS" OR KAYNAK="SU" THEN
				UrunKod(seviye+1)=	StokKodu
'				xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,18).Value =UrunKod(seviye+1)
			END IF

			xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,1).Value = seviye
			xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,2).Value = StokKodu
			xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,3).Value = ML_Table.ACIKLAMA(i)
			xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,4).Value = Miktar

			KartTipi= doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","KART_TIPI",StokKodu,"")

			xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,6).Value = ""& KartTipi

			KartBirim= doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,"")
			xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,5).Value = KartBirim

			Set G01 = Doc.Select1("STOK0GT111", "STOK0GT", "STOKCODE='"+StokKodu+"'&STATU='T'", "", -1)
			if G01.GetRowCount>0 then
				tedarikci=trim(G01.KOD(1))
				xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,7).Value = tedarikci
					if tedarikci<>"" then
						ChAD= doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",tedarikci,"")
						xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,8).Value = ChAD

						IlgiliCH = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ILGILI_CHKODU", tedarikci, "")
						if trim(IlgiliCH) <> "" Then tedarikci= IlgiliCH

						aFiy = Doc.GetStdFiyat("P","DF", "","FT_DFII", doc.BUGUN ,0,tedarikci ,StokKodu,KartBirim,"", _   
							"","", "", "", "", "",0,"","")

							if Doc.d7IsArray(aFiy) then
							  FIYAT = aFiy(0,0)    
							  PARABIRIMI = aFiy(1,0)     
								xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,9).Value = FIYAT 
								xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,10).Value = PARABIRIMI
							end if

					end if

			end if

	If KAYNAK = "OP" THEN
		xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,12).Value = (Miktar	- 	Trim(ML_Table.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i)) )*3600
		xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,13).Value = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_2", stokkodu , "")
		xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,14).Value = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_3", stokkodu , "")

			Set G01 = Doc.Select1("BOMU01T111", "BOMU01T", "BOMREC_CODE='"+UrunKod(CINT(seviye))+"'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'", "", -1)
				
			if G01.GetRowCount>0 then
				Kisi=trim(G01.OPERATORSAYISI1(1))
				xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,11).Value = Kisi
			END IF
		xl.Sheets(1).cells(satir,15).Value = 	Trim(ML_Table.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(i))
				

	END IF
			satir = satir + 1
	Next
	ExcelSheet.Visible = True

	xl.ActiveSheet.Columns(1).autofit
	Set xl = Nothing
	Set ExcelSheet = Nothing
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : MHL08 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETIKET
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK60,STOK25,STOK29,STOK68,STOK29I,TRACK25,STOK19,STOK26,STOK61,STOK63,STOK24,STOK36, MMPS10,STOKFR2,STOK46
Açýklama : ETÝKET
Kod : 
Dim Doc1, d7LB
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
'	if Doc.GetDocType <> "STOKFR2" and Doc.GetDocType <> "TRACK25" Then
'		if Doc.Save_Voucher <> 0 then
'			Exit Sub
'		End if
'	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Call STOK20_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK68" Then
		Call STOK68_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Call STOK60_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOKFR2" or Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" Then
		Call STOK25_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		Call STOK29_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK36" Then
		Call STOK36_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK19" Then
		Call STOK19_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then
		Call Transfer_Print(STOK26T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK63" Then
		Call Transfer_Print(STOK63T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK61" Then
		Call Transfer_Print(STOK61T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK24" Then
		Call STOK24_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MMPS10" Then
		Call MMPS10_Print()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK46" Then
		Call STOK46_Print()
	End if

	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "D7KIDSLB", ""
End Sub
Sub STOK20_Print()
	STOK20T.SafePack()
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2("XXXSTOK20T", "STOK20T", "", "", -1)
	Hata = ""
	S20T.GroupBy2("MPSNO")
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		Statu = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("MMPS10E", "STATUS", S20T.MPSNO(i), "")

		if (TRIM(S20T.MPSNO(i))<>"") AND (trim(Statu) <> "R") Then
			Hata = Hata & "MPS No : " & trim(S20T.MPSNO(i)) & vbcrlf
		End if
	Next
	if trim(Hata) <> "" Then
		k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Hata & "R statüsünde olmayan MPS 'lere etiket veremezsiniz.", "Hata !", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "XXXSTOK20T"

	Set S20T = Doc.Select2("XXXSTOK20T", "STOK20T", "", "", -1)
	S20T.GroupBy2("SERINO")
	for i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etiket Bilgileri Güncelleniyor : " & i & " / " & S20T.GetRowCount()
		Redim IDArray(1,0)
		StokKodu = S20T.KOD(i)
		BaskiSayisi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "BASKISAYISI", Doc.LeftJustify(S20T.SERINO(i), 30),"") 
		if trim(BaskiSayisi) > 0 Then
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "BASKISAYISI", BaskiSayisi + 1)
			S20T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = "E"
		End if
		CHKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "MUSTERIKODU", S20T.MPSNO(i) & S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i),"EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
		if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(StokKodu)
		if trim(CHKodu) <> "" Then S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = CHKodu
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "CH_MUSTERI", CHKodu)
		'UCHKodu = "99999"
		'Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "CH_TEDARIKCI", UCHKodu)
'		SevkAdresKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "SHIPTOCODE", S20T.MPSNO(i) & S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i),"EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "REFTEXT01", SevkAdresKodu)
		EtiketFormu = GetEtiketFormKodu(S20T.KOD(i), CHKodu, "")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "FORMKODU", EtiketFormu)
		S20T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(EtiketFormu),10)
		solSag = uCase(Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", S20T.KOD(i), ""))
		if Instr(1, solSag, "SAG") > 0 or Instr(1, solSag, "SAÐ") > 0 Then
			S20T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("TURUNCU",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "TURUNCU")
		Else
			S20T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("BEYAZ",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "BEYAZ")
		End if
		AmbalajKodu = GetKasaKodu(StokKodu, CHKodu, SevkAdresKodu)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "AMBALAJKODU", AmbalajKodu)

		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "MPSNO", S20T.MPSNO(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "MPSTM_TRNUM", S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SF_MIKTAR", S20T.SF_MIKTAR(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SF_UNIT", S20T.SF_SF_UNIT(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "STATU", "")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "EVRAKTYPE", "MG")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "EVRAKNO", STOK20E.EVRAKNO)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "REFTARIH1", STOK20E.TARIH)
		Call EtiketBilgileriniYaz(S20T.KOD(i), S20T.SERINO(i), S20T.LOTNUMBER(i), IDArray)
	Next
	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYYSTOK20T=STOK20T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S20T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("YYYSTOK20T")
	S20T1.CopyTable(S20T)

	S20T.GroupBy("SF_VRI_NUM2+SF_VRI_NUM1")
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "SF_VRI_NUM1='"+S20T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)+"'&SF_VRI_NUM2='"+S20T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)+"'&SF_VRI_VR_C<>'E'"
		Set PSTOK20T = Doc.Select2("PSTOK20T", "YYYSTOK20T", QStr, "", -1)
		EtiketFormu = S20T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		if trim(EtiketFormu) <> "" and PSTOK20T.GetRowCount > 0 Then
			par1="PSTOK20T"
			Doc.RunMacro1 "PFRM7S." & trim(EtiketFormu),"PrintLabel1", par1
			Call EtiketleriGuncelle_STOK20(PSTOK20T)
		End if
	Next
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "XXXSTOK20T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "YYYSTOK20T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "PSTOK20T"
End Sub
Sub STOK68_Print()
	STOK68T.SafePack()
	Set S68T = Doc.Select2("XXXSTOK68T", "STOK68T", "", "", -1)
	S68T.GroupBy2("SERINO")
	for i = 1 to S68T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etiket Bilgileri Güncelleniyor : " & i & " / " & S68T.GetRowCount()
		Redim IDArray(1,0)
		StokKodu = S68T.KOD(i)
		BaskiSayisi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "BASKISAYISI", Doc.LeftJustify(S68T.SERINO(i), 30),"") 
		if trim(BaskiSayisi) > 0 Then
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "BASKISAYISI", BaskiSayisi + 1)
			S68T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = "E"
		End if
		if trim(S68T.MUSTERI_CH_KODU(i)) <> "" Then
			CHKodu = S68T.MUSTERI_CH_KODU(i)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "EVRAKTYPE", "MG")
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "APPKODU", "STOK20")
		Else
			UCHKodu = STOK68E.CHCODE
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "EVRAKTYPE", "FG")
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "APPKODU", "STOK68")
		End if
		if trim(CHKodu) <> "" Then S68T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = CHKodu
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "CH_MUSTERI", CHKodu)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "CH_TEDARIKCI", UCHKodu)
'		SevkAdresKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "SHIPTOCODE", S68T.MPSNO(i) & S68T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i),"EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "REFTEXT01", SevkAdresKodu)
		EtiketFormu = GetEtiketFormKodu(S68T.KOD(i), CHKodu, "")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "FORMKODU", EtiketFormu)
		S68T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(EtiketFormu),10)
		solSag = uCase(Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", S68T.KOD(i), ""))
		if Instr(1, solSag, "SAG") > 0 or Instr(1, solSag, "SAÐ") > 0 Then
			S68T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("TURUNCU",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "TURUNCU")
		Else
			S68T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("BEYAZ",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "BEYAZ")
		End if
		AmbalajKodu = GetKasaKodu(StokKodu, CHKodu, SevkAdresKodu)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "AMBALAJKODU", AmbalajKodu)

		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "MPSNO", S68T.MPSNO(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "MPSTM_TRNUM", S68T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SF_MIKTAR", S68T.SF_MIKTAR(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SF_UNIT", S68T.SF_SF_UNIT(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "STATU", "")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "EVRAKNO", STOK68E.EVRAKNO)
'		S68T.EVRAKNO(i) = STOK68E.XIRSALIYENO
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "REFTARIH1", STOK68E.TARIH)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SIPARISNO_46", S68T.SIPNO(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SIPARTNO_46", S68T.SIPARTNO(i))

		Call EtiketBilgileriniYaz(S68T.KOD(i), S68T.SERINO(i), S68T.LOTNUMBER(i), IDArray)
	Next
	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYYSTOK68T=STOK68T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S68T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("YYYSTOK68T")
	S68T1.CopyTable(S68T)

	S68T.GroupBy("SF_VRI_NUM2+SF_VRI_NUM1")
	For i = 1 to S68T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "SF_VRI_NUM1='"+S68T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)+"'&SF_VRI_NUM2='"+S68T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)+"'&SF_VRI_VR_C<>'E'"
		Set PSTOK68T = Doc.Select2("PSTOK68T", "YYYSTOK68T", QStr, "", -1)
		EtiketFormu = S68T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		if trim(EtiketFormu) <> "" and PSTOK68T.GetRowCount > 0 Then
			par1="PSTOK68T"
			Doc.RunMacro1 "PFRM7S." & trim(EtiketFormu),"PrintLabel1", par1
			Call EtiketleriGuncelle_STOK68(PSTOK68T)
		End if
	Next
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "XXXSTOK68T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "YYYSTOK68T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "PSTOK68T"
End Sub
Sub STOK60_Print()
	STOK60T.SafePack()
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("XXXSTOK60T", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
	S60T.GroupBy2("SERINO")

	for i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etiket Bilgileri Güncelleniyor : " & i & " / " & S60T.GetRowCount()
		Redim IDArray(1,0)
		StokKodu = S60T.KOD(i)
		CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
		S60T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = CHKodu
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "CH_MUSTERI", CHKodu)
		EtiketFormu = GetEtiketFormKodu(S60T.KOD(i), CHKodu, "")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "FORMKODU", EtiketFormu)
		S60T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(EtiketFormu),10)
		solSag = uCase(Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", S60T.KOD(i), ""))
		if Instr(1, solSag, "SAG") > 0 or Instr(1, solSag, "SAÐ") > 0 Then
			S60T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("TURUNCU",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "TURUNCU")
		Else
			S60T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("BEYAZ",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "BEYAZ")
		End if
		AmbalajKodu = GetKasaKodu(StokKodu, CHKodu, SevkAdresKodu)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "AMBALAJKODU", AmbalajKodu)

		Call EtiketBilgileriniYaz(S60T.KOD(i), S60T.SERINO(i), S60T.LOTNUMBER(i), IDArray)
	Next

	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYYSTOK60T=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S60T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("YYYSTOK60T")
	S60T1.CopyTable(S60T)

	S60T.GroupBy("SF_VRI_NUM2+SF_VRI_NUM1")
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "SF_VRI_NUM1='"+S60T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)+"'&SF_VRI_NUM2='"+S60T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)+"'"
		Set PSTOK60T = Doc.Select2("PSTOK60T", "YYYSTOK60T", QStr, "", -1)
		EtiketFormu = trim(S60T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i))
		if trim(EtiketFormu) <> "" Then
			par1="PSTOK60T"
			Doc.RunMacro1 "PFRM7S." & trim(EtiketFormu),"PrintLabel1", par1
'			Call EtiketleriGuncelle_STOK60(PSTOK60T)			
		End if
	Next
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "XXXSTOK60T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "YYYSTOK60T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "PSTOK60T"
End Sub
Sub STOK29_Print()
	STOK29T.SafePack()
	Set S29T = Doc.Select2("XXXSTOK29T", "STOK29T", "", "", -1)
	S29T.GroupBy2("SERINO")
	for i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etiket Bilgileri Güncelleniyor : " & i & " / " & S29T.GetRowCount()
		Redim IDArray(1,0)
		StokKodu = S29T.KOD(i)
		BaskiSayisi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "BASKISAYISI", Doc.LeftJustify(S29T.SERINO(i), 30),"") 
		if BaskiSayisi > 0 Then
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "BASKISAYISI", BaskiSayisi + 1)
			S29T.SF_VRI_VR_C(i) = "E"
		End if
		if trim(S29T.MUSTERI_CH_KODU(i)) <> "" Then
			CHKodu = S29T.MUSTERI_CH_KODU(i)
		Else
			UCHKodu = STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE
		End if
		if trim(CHKodu) <> "" Then S29T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = CHKodu
		if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "REFTEXT02", trim(STOK29E.NITELIK))
		End if
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "CH_MUSTERI", CHKodu)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "CH_TEDARIKCI", UCHKodu)
		EtiketFormu = GetEtiketFormKodu(S29T.KOD(i), CHKodu, "")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "FORMKODU", EtiketFormu)
		S29T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(EtiketFormu),10)
		solSag = uCase(Doc.GetDBFieldvalue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", S29T.KOD(i), ""))
		if Instr(1, solSag, "SAG") > 0 or Instr(1, solSag, "SAÐ") > 0 Then
			S29T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("TURUNCU",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "TURUNCU")
		Else
			S29T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i) = Doc.RightJustify("BEYAZ",10)
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "GK_1", "BEYAZ")
		End if
		AmbalajKodu = GetKasaKodu(StokKodu, CHKodu, SevkAdresKodu)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "AMBALAJKODU", AmbalajKodu)

		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SF_MIKTAR", S29T.SF_MIKTAR(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SF_UNIT", S29T.SF_SF_UNIT(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "STATU", "")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "EVRAKTYPE", "SI")
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "EVRAKNO", STOK29E.EVRAKNO)
'		S29T.EVRAKNO(i) = STOK29E.XIRSALIYENO
'		if trim(STOK29E.XIRSALIYETARIH) <> "" Then
'			IrsaliyeTarihi = STOK29E.XIRSALIYETARIH
'		Else
			IrsaliyeTarihi = STOK29E.TARIH
'		End if
		S29T.TARIH(i) = IrsaliyeTarihi
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "REFTARIH1", IrsaliyeTarihi)
		'Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "UD_CR_TAR", Doc.BuGun)
		'Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "UD_EVCHKOD", STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE)
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SIPARISNO_46", S29T.SIPNO(i))
		Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "SIPARTNO_46", S29T.SIPARTNO(i))

		Call EtiketBilgileriniYaz(S29T.KOD(i), S29T.SERINO(i), S29T.LOTNUMBER(i), IDArray)
	Next

	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYYSTOK29T=STOK29T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S29T1 = Doc.GetTableObject("YYYSTOK29T")
	S29T1.CopyTable(S29T)
	For i = 1 to S29T1.GetRowCount()
		S29T1.AMBCODE(i) = GetS01Value(S29T1.SERINO(i), "AMBCODE")
	Next

	S29T.GroupBy("SF_VRI_NUM2+SF_VRI_NUM1")
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "SF_VRI_NUM1='"+S29T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)+"'&SF_VRI_NUM2='"+S29T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)+"'&SF_VRI_VR_C<>'E'"
'		QStr = "SF_VRI_NUM1='"+S29T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)+"'&SF_VRI_NUM2='"+S29T.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)+"'"
		Set PSTOK29T = Doc.Select2("PSTOK29T", "YYYSTOK29T", QStr, "", -1)
		EtiketFormu = S29T.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		if trim(EtiketFormu) <> "" and PSTOK29T.GetRowCount > 0 Then
			par1="PSTOK29T"
			Doc.RunMacro1 "PFRM7S." & trim(EtiketFormu),"PrintLabel1", par1
			Call EtiketleriGuncelle_STOK29(PSTOK29T)
		End if
	Next
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "XXXSTOK29T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "YYYSTOK29T"
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "PSTOK29T"
End Sub
Sub STOK25_Print()
'	if Doc.Save_Voucher() <> 0 Then Exit Sub
	KoliBolYaz = False
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXXSTOK20T25=STOK20T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S20T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXXSTOK20T25")

	Set S25E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	if Doc.GetDocType() = "STOKFR2" or Doc.GetDocType() = "TRACK25" Then
		if not isObject(STACK) Then
			Set STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK")
		End if
		if trim(STACK.SON_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
			Set S25T = Doc.Select1("STOK25TPR","STOK25T", "EVRAKNO='"+trim(STACK.SON_EVRAKNO)+"'", "", -1)
			Set S25E = Doc.Select1("STOK25EPR","STOK25E", "EVRAKNO='"+trim(STACK.SON_EVRAKNO)+"'", "", -1)
			if S25T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
		Else
			Redim IDArray(1,0)
			KoliBolYaz = True
			if trim(STOK01.SERINO) = "" Then
				k = Doc.d7MsgBox("Etiket Seçmediniz", "dinamo", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING)
				Exit Sub
			End if
			S20T.AddRow
			S20T.KOD(1) = STOK01.KOD
			S20T.LOTNUMBER(1) = STOK01.LOTNUMBER
			S20T.SERINO(1) = STOK01.SERINO
			S20T.SF_MIKTAR(1) = STOK01.SF_MIKTAR
			S20T.SF_SF_UNIT(1) = STOK01.SF_UNIT

			S20T.FOKOD(1) = STOK01.FOKOD
			S20T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(1) = STOK01.VRI_VR_R1
		
			S20T.EVRAKNO(1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "EVRAKNO", Doc.LeftJustify(STOK01.SERINO, 30),"") 
			S20T.TARIH(1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "REFTARIH1", Doc.LeftJustify(STOK01.SERINO, 30),"") 
			S20T.MPSNO(1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "MPSNO", Doc.LeftJustify(STOK01.SERINO, 30),"") 

			CHKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "CH_MUSTERI", Doc.LeftJustify(STOK01.SERINO, 30),"") 
			EtiketFormu = GetEtiketFormKodu(STOK01.KOD, CHKodu, "")
			
			Call IDArrayAdd(IDArray, "FORMKODU", EtiketFormu)
			S20T.SF_VRI_NUM1(1) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(EtiketFormu),10)
			solSag = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("d7KIDSLB", "GK_1", Doc.LeftJustify(STOK01.SERINO, 30),"") 

			S20T.SF_VRI_NUM2(1) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(EtiketFormu),10)
			S20T.SF_VRI_NUM1(1) = Doc.RightJustify(trim(solSag), 10)
			Set S01 = Doc.Select1("QWQWQW", "STOK01", "SERINO='"+STOK01.SERINO+"'", "", -1)
			if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				S20T.AMBCODE(1) = S01.AMBCODE(1)
				S20T.LOCATION1(1) = S01.LOCATION1(1)
				S20T.LOCATION2(1) = S01.LOCATION2(1)
				S20T.LOCATION3(1) = S01.LOCATION3(1)
				S20T.LOCATION4(1) = S01.LOCATION4(1)
			End if
			Call EtiketBilgileriniYaz(STOK01.KOD, STOK01.SERINO, STOK01.LOTNUMBER, IDArray)
		End if
	End if
	if not KoliBolYaz Then
		S25T.SafePack()
'yeni seriler
		for i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etiket Bilgileri Güncelleniyor : " & i & " / " & S25T.GetRowCount()
			Redim IDArray(1,0)
			S20T.AddRow()
			satirNo = S20T.GetRowCount()

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, MainTableT
Sub PrintLabel1(TABLO)
	Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject(TABLO)
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 6
	EtiketBoyu = 112
	EtiketEni = 192
	EtiketlerArasi = 120
	solMargin = 9
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 9

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

'	STOK20T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to MainTableT.GetRecCount()
		Miktar = MainTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)	
'		StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(i)
'		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
'		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
'		if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
'		EtiketAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Miktar / KutuIciMiktar)
'		for j = 1 to EtiketAdedi
'			Kalan = Miktar - (j-1) * KutuIciMiktar
'			if Kalan < KutuIciMiktar then
'				KutuMiktari = Kalan
'			else
'				KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar
'			end if
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
			yEtiketSayisi = (EtiketNo-1) mod 2
			if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 and EtiketNo > 1 then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()
				Ust = Ustmargin					
			else
				Ust = Ustmargin + yEtiketSayisi * EtiketlerArasi
			end if
'			Call Etiket(pe1,i, KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
			Call Etiket(pe1,i, Miktar, EtiketAdedi)
'		Next
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo,KutuMiktari, EtiketAdedi)
	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 160)
	solSag = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(120,120,120))
'	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 20, solMargin, 80, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(solSag))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = solMargin + 27.5
	D2 = solMargin + 85
	D3 = solMargin + 126.5
	D4 = solMargin + 137

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 20.5
	Y2 = Ust + 28.5
	Y3 = Ust + 37
	Y4 = Ust + 46.5
	Y5 = Ust + 56
	Y6 = Ust + 65
	Y7 = Ust + 79
	Y8 = Ust + 89
	Y9 = Ust + 100

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.2)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, solMargin, Alt - Ust, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, solMargin + 9, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 9)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, solMargin + 9, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 9)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, solMargin + 9, 0, sagMargin - solMargin - 9)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, solMargin + 9, 0, D4 - solMargin - 9)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, solMargin + 9, 0, D4 - solMargin - 9)
	res = pe1.Line(Y9, solMargin, 0, D2 - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y9, D4, 0, sagMargin - D4)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Y1 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D3, Y1 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, D2, Alt - Y8, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, D4, Alt - Y6, 0)

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ust + 3, solmargin + 1, Y1 - Ust - 3, D1 - solMargin - 2, logo, 114)

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	SeriNo = trim(MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = 50
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust + 3.5, D3 + 4, 8, BarkodGen, "CODE39", SeriNo, "")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 13.5, D3 + 4, 6, BarkodGen, "*" & Serino & "*")

	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 30)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 2, D1, 15, D3 - D1, trim(StokKodu))

	Genislik10 = 24
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y1 + 2, solMargin + 1, 6, 21, "Parça Adý")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, 88, StokAdi)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 9)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop , solMargin + 105, 6, 15, "D. Lot No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight, 6, 20, MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop , solMargin + 142, 6, Genislik10, "TEDARIKCI. Lot No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight, MainTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo))


	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dsdfwe", "D7KIDSLB", "SERINO='"+SeriNo+"'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	EvrakTuru = d7LB.EVRAKTYPE
	IadeNitelik = d7LB.REFTEXT02
	if trim(IadeNitelik) <> "" and Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		EvrakTuruAciklama = "Satýþ Ýade Ýrsaliyesi"
		Nitelik = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKNIT", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(IadeNitelik), 10), -1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(Y4 + 2, solMargin + 11.5, 6, 100, Nitelik)
	Else
		EvrakTuruAciklama = Doc.GetGecoValue("|D7KET", "AD", EvrakTuru, -1)
	End if
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Y2 + 2, solmargin + 1, 6, 25, "Oluþma Þekli")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, 6, 63, EvrakTuru & " - " & EvrakTuruAciklama)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, 12, "Tarih")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, 40, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun()))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 142, 6, Genislik10, "Belge")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight, trim(MainTableT.EVRAKNO))

'	UCHKodu = Doc.GetQValue(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo),MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo),"","UD_UCH_KOD",0) 
	UCHKodu = d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI
	if trim(UCHKodu) = "" Then
		UCHAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		UCHStokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	Else
		UCHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", UCHKodu, "")
		UCHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu, UCHKodu)
	End if
	EvrakTarihi = MainTableT.TARIH(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y3 + 2, solMargin + 10, 6, 15, "Firma")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	UCHAdi_1 = LEFT(UCHAdi,75)

'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, 115, UCHKodu  & UCHAdi)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, 115, UCHKodu  & UCHAdi_1)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 142, 6, Genislik10, "Belge Tarihi")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight, Doc.StrToNorDate(EvrakTarihi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y5 + 2, solMargin + 5, 6, 17, "Orj. Kodu")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 8, D2 - pe1.GetRight, trim(UCHStokKodu))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y4 + 2, solMargin + 142, 6, Genislik10, "Ýþ Emri No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
'	MPSNo = Doc.GetQValue(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo),MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo),"","UD_MPSNO",0)
	MPSNo = d7LB.MPSNO
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight, MPSNo)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y5 + 2, solMargin + 82, 6, 24, "Üretim Birimi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, 36, "")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y5 + 2, solMargin + 142, 6, Genislik10, "Orj. Seri No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 11)
'	OrjSeriNo = Doc.GetQValue(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo),MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo),"","UD_OSERINO",0)
	OrjSeriNo = d7LB.ILKETIKET
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 6, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight, OrjSeriNo)
	
'	CHKodu = Doc.GetQValue(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo),MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo),"","UD_CH_KOD",0) 
	CHKodu = d7LB.CH_MUSTERI
'	AdresKodu = Doc.GetQValue(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo),MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo),"","UD_UCH_KOD",0) 
	AdresKodu = d7LB.REFTEXT01

	if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
		CHAdi = Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0")
		CHStokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	Else
		CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
'		CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu, CHKodu)
		CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHIVal(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo), CHKodu,AdresKodu,"MSTOKCODE")
	End if

	IF TRIM(MainTableT.FOKOD(satirNo)) <> ""  THEN 
			FOKOD= 	MainTableT.FOKOD(satirNo)
			FOKOD_AD= Doc.MakeFONam(FOKod,1,50,1,1)
			CHAdi= FOKOD & " " & TRIM(FOKOD_AD)	
	END IF


	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y6 + 2, solMargin + 2, 6, 30, "Oluþum Amacý")
	res = pe1.Write(Y6 + 8, solMargin + 11.5, 6, 15, "Firma")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 8, D4 - pe1.GetRight - 1, trim(CHKodu) & "   " & CHAdi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y8 + 2, solMargin + 5, 6, 17, "Orj. Kodu")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 8, D2 - pe1.GetRight, trim(CHStokKodu))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	'AmbalajKodu = Doc.GetQValue(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo),MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo),"","UD_KASATUR",0) 
	AmbalajKodu = d7LB.AMBALAJKODU
	AmbalajAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", AmbalajKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y9 + 2, solMargin + 2, 5, 35, "Ambalaj Açýklamasý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, D2 - pe1.GetLeft - 1, trim(AmbalajKodu) & " " & AmbalajAdi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(Y8 + 2, D2 + 2, 5, 35, "Miktar")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 24)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, D2, 12, D4 - D2 - 2, KutuMiktari)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 10.5, D2, 8, D4 - D2 - 2, trim(MainTableT.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	if trim(d7LB.APPKODU) = "MMPS10" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
		res = pe1.Write(Y6 + 12, D4, 15, sagMargin - D4, "UYGUN")
	Else
		res = pe1.Write(Y6 + 12, D4, 7, sagMargin - D4, "KONTROL")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, D4, 7, sagMargin - D4, "BEKLÝYOR")
	End if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(Y9 + 1, D4 + 1, 6, 15, "Depo")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 1, Doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00", "AD", MainTableT.AMBCODE(satirNo), ""))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, D4 + 1, 6, 25, "Lokasyon")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	Lokasyon = trim(MainTableT.LOCATION1(satirNo)) & "-" & trim(MainTableT.LOCATION2(satirNo)) & trim(MainTableT.LOCATION3(satirNo))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.getRight, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 1, Lokasyon)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(Alt + 0.5, solMargin + 1, 5, 30, Doc.Getd7UserName)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu)
	Set T_STOK0GT = Doc.Select1("XXX","STOK0GT", "STOKCODE='"+StokKodu+"'","", -1) 
	if T_STOK0GT.GetRecCount = 0 then
		GetCHStokKodu = "" 
		exit Function
	else
		GetCHStokKodu = T_STOK0GT.MSTOKCODE(1)
	end if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject("XXX")
End Function
Sub DikeyYaz(pe1, Ust, Sol, En, Yazi, Aralik)
	Punto = pe1.GetFontSize
	YaziYuk = Punto * 0.25 * 1.8
	if Aralik = -1 then Aralik = YaziYuk * 0.8
	for i = 1 to Len(Yazi)
		Harf = Mid(Yazi, i, 1)
		Ust1 = Ust + (i - 1) * Aralik
		res = pe1.Write(Ust1, sol, YaziYuk, En, Harf)
	Next
End Sub
Function GetCHKodu(StokKodu)
	Set T_STOK0GT = Doc.Select1("XXX","STOK0GT", "STOKCODE='"+StokKodu+"'","", -1) 
	if T_STOK0GT.GetRecCount = 0 then
		GetCHKodu = "" 
		exit Function
	else
		GetCHKodu = T_STOK0GT.KOD(1)
	end if
	Doc.DeleteTableObject("XXX")
End Function

'	Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject(TABLO)
	Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject(STOK60E)

	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "MAHLE FARPLAS FÝLTRE SÝSTEMLERÝ A.Þ."
	FirmaKodu = "FAHNA"

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 148
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 143
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoChecksum(0)

	
	Set MainTableT = Doc.Select2("asda", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
'	MainTableT.GroupBy("BRUTAGIRLIK")

	For i = 1 to MainTableT.GetRecCount()
		for k = 1 to 1 'her etiketten 2 tane
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
'			if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
				Ust = UstMargin
				if EtiketNo <> 1 then
					res = pe1.PageBreak()
				end if
'			else
'				Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
'			end if
			Call Etiket(pe1,i)
		next
	Next

	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo)

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
'		Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
'		Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	End if

	StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 120)
	'solSag = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(120,120,120))
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 25, solMargin, 80, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(solSag))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)


	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 95
	D2 = D1 + 36
	D3 = D1 + 48
	D4 = D1 + 72

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 20
	Y2 = Y1 + 13
	Y3 = Y2 + 13
	Y4 = Y3 + 30
	Y5 = Y4 + 10.5
	Y6 = Y4 + 28
	Y7 = Y5 + 28
	Y8 = Y6 + 21
	Y9 = Y8 + 23

	res = pe1.Box(Ust, SolMargin, Y9 - Ust, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, SolMargin, 0, D1 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Y3 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D2, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D4, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, D1, Alt - Y4, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D3, Y8 - Y7, 0)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust + 1


	DockCode = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_1",MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),""))
	MarketPlace = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_2",MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),""))
	Revizyon = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_3",MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),""))


	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
	'	Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE 			'LOC+11
	'	Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1
		Alici1 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1
	Else
		Alici1 = "FORD MOTOR CO."
	End if

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	Else
		CHKodu = Doc.GetQValue(StokKodu, "", MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo), "", "UD_CH_KOD", 0)
		Alici1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	End if
	CHStokKodu = trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),CHKodu))


	DockCode  = TRIM(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_1", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" ))
	MarketPlace = TRIM(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_2", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" ))

	Loc159= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_1", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" )



	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, SolMargin + 2.5 ,4,18,"ALICI")  'LOC+11
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",24)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, SolMargin + 6,18, sag - Sol+12, trim(Alici1))
		res = pe1.Write(rUst +2, SolMargin + 1,18, sag - Sol+12, trim(Alici1))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, SolMargin + 2,10, sag - Sol,trim(Alici2))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 7, SolMargin + 2,8, sag - Sol,trim(Alici3))

	sol = D1 + 1

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 52)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, sol, 30, sag - sol, trim(DockCode + "   "+ MarketPlace))

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		IrsaliyeNo = trim(STOK60E.EXTERNALEVRAKNO)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",24)

	sag = sagMargin

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	Else

	End if
	CHStokKodu = trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),CHKodu))

	CHStokAdi  = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT", "MSTOKNAME", MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)+CHKodu, "STOKCODE+KOD")

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+16,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	End if

	GuvenlikParcasi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_11", MainTableT.KOD(satirNo), "")
	if GuvenlikParcasi = 1 Then
		resim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "images\guvenlik.jpg"
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()

	sol = D1
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y4

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 1,4,sag - sol - 1,"PARÇA TANIMI")

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 1,15,sag - sol - 1,ParcaAdi)
	End if

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D3
	rUst = Y7 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1, 5, 12, "SEVK TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 16, sag - sol, "D" & Replace(Mid(STOK60E.TARIH, 3),"/",""))
	End if

	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y7 + 1

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 36, "ÝNDÝS NO")

'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.Getright, 15, sag - pe1.Getright, DegisiklikTarihi)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 16, sag - sol, Revizyon)
	End if
	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y4

	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PaletMiktari)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_4",StokKodu,"") * MainTableT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Round(NetAgirlik, 0)
End Function
Function GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	GetBrutAgirlik = NetAgirlik + KasaAgirligi
End Function
Function GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",StokKodu,"")
	GetKasaAgirligi = Doc.Round(KasaAgirligi, 0)
End Function

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi, MainTableT
Sub PrintLabel1(TABLO)
'	Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject(TABLO)
	Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject(STOK60E)

	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "MAHLE FARPLAS FÝLTRE SÝSTEMLERÝ A.Þ."
	FirmaKodu = "FAHNA"

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 140
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 145
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoChecksum(0)

	
	Set MainTableT = Doc.Select2("asda", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
'	MainTableT.GroupBy("BRUTAGIRLIK")

	For i = 1 to MainTableT.GetRecCount()
		for k = 1 to 1 'her etiketten 2 tane
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
			if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
				Ust = UstMargin
				if EtiketNo <> 1 then
					res = pe1.PageBreak()
				end if
			else
				Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
			end if
			Call Etiket(pe1,i)
		next
	Next

	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo)

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
'		Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
'		Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	End if

	StokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(satirNo)

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 120)
	'solSag = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(120,120,120))
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 25, solMargin, 80, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(solSag))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)


	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 95
	D2 = D1 + 36
	D3 = D1 + 48
	D4 = D1 + 72

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 20
	Y2 = Y1 + 13
	Y3 = Y2 + 13
	Y4 = Y3 + 29   '30
	Y5 = Y4 + 10.5
	Y6 = Y4 + 25	'28
	Y7 = Y5 + 26  '28
	Y8 = Y6 + 20	'21
	Y9 = Y8 + 20  '23 

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, SolMargin, Y9 - Ust, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, SolMargin, 0, D1 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Y3 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D2, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D4, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, D1, Alt - Y4, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D3, Y8 - Y7, 0)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust + 1


	DockCode = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_1",MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),""))
	MarketPlace = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_2",MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),""))
	Revizyon = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_3",MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),""))


	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
	'	Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE 			'LOC+11
	'	Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1
		Alici1 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1
	Else
		Alici1 = "FORD MOTOR CO."
	End if

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	Else
		CHKodu = Doc.GetQValue(StokKodu, "", MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo), "", "UD_CH_KOD", 0)
		Alici1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	End if
	CHStokKodu = trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),CHKodu))


	DockCode  = TRIM(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_1", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" ))
	MarketPlace = TRIM(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_2", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" ))

	Loc159= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_1", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" )



	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, SolMargin + 2.5 ,4,18,"ALICI")  'LOC+11
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",24)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst +2, SolMargin + 1,18, sag - Sol+12, trim(Alici1))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, SolMargin + 2,10, sag - Sol,trim(Alici2))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 7, SolMargin + 2,8, sag - Sol,trim(Alici3))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1,4,50,"KAMYON ÝNDÝRME BÖLGESÝ") 		'LOC+159
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 45)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, sol, 30, sag - sol, trim(DockCode + "   "+ MarketPlace))

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		IrsaliyeNo = trim(STOK60E.EXTERNALEVRAKNO)
	End if
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(IrsaliyeNo)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝRSALÝYE NO (N)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",24)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 2,28,sag - sol,IrsaliyeNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+9,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","N" & trim(IrsaliyeNo) & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝMALATÇI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 16)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 16, sag - sol, FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Adres)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	Else
		CHKodu = Doc.GetQValue(StokKodu, "", MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo), "", "UD_CH_KOD", 0)
	End if
	CHStokKodu = trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),CHKodu))

	CHStokAdi  = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK0GT", "MSTOKNAME", MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)+CHKodu, "STOKCODE+KOD")

	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(rUst - 4, sol1 + 10,25,sag - sol1 - 10,CHStokKodu)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	End if

	GuvenlikParcasi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_11", MainTableT.KOD(satirNo), "")
	if GuvenlikParcasi = 1 Then
		resim = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "images\guvenlik.jpg"
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.Picture(rUst + 5, sag - 45, 40, 40, resim, 114)
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	End if

	sol = D1
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y4
	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo),CHKodu)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 1,4,sag - sol - 1,"PARÇA TANIMI")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 1,15,sag - sol - 1,ParcaAdi)
	End if

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D3
	rUst = Y7 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1, 5, 12, "SEVK TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop-1.5 , sol, 16, sag - sol, "D" & Replace(Mid(STOK60E.TARIH, 3),"/",""))
	End if

	sol = D3 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y7 + 1

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 36, "ÝNDÝS NO")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.Getright, 15, sag - pe1.Getright, DegisiklikTarihi)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 16, sag - sol, Revizyon)
	End if
	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y4
	PaletMiktari = MainTableT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PaletMiktari)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"MÝKTAR (Q)")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",44)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(rUst - 4, sol1,25, sag - sol1, PaletMiktari)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+12,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & PaletMiktari & "","")
	End if

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	PaletNetAgirligi = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "NET AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(PaletNetAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = D4
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	PaletBrutAgirligi = GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "BRÜT AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(PaletBrutAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D4 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	KasaAdedi = 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "KUTU MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KasaAdedi,15,0))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y5 + 1
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(StokKodu)
	sol1 = sol + 5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 30, "ÝMALATÇI PARÇA NO")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, sol1, 20, sag - sol1, trim(StokKodu))
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+11,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39",trim(StokKodu),"")
	End if

	sol = solmargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y6
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(FirmaKodu)
	sol1 = Sol + 10

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝMALATÇI KODU (V)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1.5, sol1 + 10,10,sag - sol1, FirmaKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+5,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","V" & FirmaKodu,"")

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y8
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	SeriNo = trim(MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(SeriNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ETÝKET NO (S)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol1 + 10,10,sag - sol1, SeriNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+7,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","S" & SeriNo,"")

	sol = D1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y8
	PartiNo = trim(MainTableT.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PartiNo)
	sol1 = Sol + 5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"LOT NUMARASI (H)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1.5, sol1 + 15,10,sag - sol1 - 15, PartiNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	if trim(PartiNo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+6,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","H" & trim(PartiNo),"")
	End if
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(Y9 - 4, sag - 32,5,32, "ODETTE Ver.1 Rev.4")

End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_4",StokKodu,"") * MainTableT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Round(NetAgirlik, 0)
End Function
Function GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	GetBrutAgirlik = NetAgirlik + KasaAgirligi
End Function
Function GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",StokKodu,"")
	GetKasaAgirligi = Doc.Round(KasaAgirligi, 0)
End Function

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi, S60T
Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "MAHLE FARPLAS"
	FirmaKodu = "GÝRÝLECEK"

	STOK60T.SafePack()
	for i = 1 to STOK60T.GetRecCount()
'		stokKodu = STOK60T.KOD(i)
		Ambalaj = STOK60T.FATURASIZMI(i)
		if trim(Ambalaj) <> "E" then
'			KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
'			KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,6)
'			if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then
'				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " sandýk içi miktar girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 1)" & vbcrlf
'			end if
'			PaletBasinaKasaAdedi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_2",StokKodu,"")
'			PaletBasinaKasaAdedi = doc.Round(PaletBasinaKasaAdedi,6)
'			if PaletBasinaKasaAdedi = 0 then
'				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " palet baþýna kasa adedi girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 2)" & vbcrlf
'			end if		
'			KasaNetAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_4",StokKodu,"")
'			KasaNetAgirligi = doc.Round(KasaNetAgirligi,6)
'			if KasaNetAgirligi = 0 then
'				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " parça aðýrlýðý girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 4)" & vbcrlf
'			end if		
'			KasaBrutAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",StokKodu,"")
'			KasaBrutAgirligi = doc.Round(KasaBrutAgirligi,6)
'			if KasaBrutAgirligi = 0 then
'				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " kasa aðýrlýðý girilmemiþ. (Stok Kartý Nümerik Taným - 5)" & vbcrlf
'			end if		
			if trim(STOK60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(i)) = "" Then
				Hata = Hata & i & ". satýr " & stokKodu & " palet numarasý girilmemiþ. (Brüt Aðýrlýk)" & vbcrlf
			End if
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Hata) <> "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(Hata & vbcrlf & "Ýþlem tamamlanmadan kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 148
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 143
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(STOK60E.EVRAKNO), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoChecksum(0)

	
	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("asda", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
	S60T.GroupBy("BRUTAGIRLIK")
	For i = 1 to S60T.GetRecCount()
		for k = 1 to 1 'her etiketten 1 tane
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
'			if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
				Ust = UstMargin
				if EtiketNo <> 1 then
					res = pe1.PageBreak()
				end if
'			else
'				Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
'			end if
			Call Etiket(pe1,i)
		next
	Next

	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo)
	StokKodu = S60T.KOD(satirNo)
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 95
	D2 = D1 + 36
	D3 = D1 + 48
	D4 = D1 + 72

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 20
	Y2 = Y1 + 13
	Y3 = Y2 + 13
	Y4 = Y3 + 30
	Y5 = Y4 + 10.5
	Y6 = Y4 + 28
	Y7 = Y5 + 28
	Y8 = Y6 + 21
	Y9 = Y8 + 23

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, SolMargin, Y9 - Ust, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, SolMargin, 0, D1 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Y3 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D2, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D4, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, D1, Alt - Y4, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D3, Y8 - Y7, 0)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust + 1
	Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE
	Alici2 = STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1
	Alici3 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, SolMargin + 2.5,4,20,"ALICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 2, SolMargin + 12,18, sag - Sol, trim(Alici1))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, SolMargin + 2,10, sag - Sol,trim(Alici2))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 7, SolMargin + 2,8, sag - Sol,trim(Alici3))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1,4,50,"KAMYON ÝNDÝRME BÖLGESÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 52)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, sol, 30, sag - sol, trim(STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_2))

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	IrsaliyeNo = right("00000000" & trim(STOK60E.EXTERNALEVRAKNO),8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(IrsaliyeNo)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝRSALÝYE NO (N)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 2,28,sag - sol,IrsaliyeNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
'	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+9,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","N" & trim(IrsaliyeNo) & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝMALATÇI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol, FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Adres)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3
	CHStokKodu = trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S60T.KOD(satirNo),STOK60E.CH_KODU))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(rUst - 2, sol1 + 10,25,sag - sol1,CHStokKodu)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+16,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	End if

	sol = D1
	sag = sagmargin
	rUst = Y4
'	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
	ParcaAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(S60T.KOD(satirNo),STOK60E.CH_KODU)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 1,4,sag - sol - 1,"PARÇA TANIMI")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",28)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1, sol + 1,15,sag - sol - 1,ParcaAdi)
	End if

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D3
	rUst = Y7 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1, 5, 12, "SEVK TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 16, sag - sol, "D" & Replace(Mid(STOK60E.TARIH, 3),"/",""))

	sol = D3 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y7 + 1
	Revizyon = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","TEXT_TECH_3",S60T.KOD(satirNo),""))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 36, "ÝNDÝS NO")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.Getright, 15, sag - pe1.Getright, DegisiklikTarihi)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop - 0.5, sol, 16, sag - sol, Revizyon)
	End if
	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y4
	PaletMiktari = S60T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PaletMiktari)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"MÝKTAR (Q)")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(rUst - 4, sol1,25, sag - sol1, PaletMiktari)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+14,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","Q" & PaletMiktari & "","")
	End if

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = D2
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	PaletNetAgirligi = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "NET AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(PaletNetAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D2 + 1
	sag = D4
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	PaletBrutAgirligi = GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "BRÜT AÐIRLIK (KG)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(PaletBrutAgirligi,15,0))

	sol = D4 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y2 + 1
	KasaAdedi = GetKasaAdedi(satirNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, sag - sol, "KUTU MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 0.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Doc.NumFormat(KasaAdedi,15,0))


	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y5 + 1
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(StokKodu)
	sol1 = sol + 5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol, 5, 30, "ÝMALATÇI PARÇA NO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, sol1, 20, sag - sol1, trim(StokKodu))
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+11,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39",trim(StokKodu),"")

	sol = solmargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y6
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(FirmaKodu)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝMALATÇI KODU (V)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1.5, sol1 + 10,10,sag - sol1, FirmaKodu)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+5,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","V" & FirmaKodu,"")

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y8
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		MonoGroup = "M"
	Else
		MonoGroup = "G"
	End if
	PaletNo = trim(S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PaletNo)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ETÝKET NO (" & MonoGroup & ")")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 0.5, sol1 + 10,10,sag - sol1, PaletNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+8,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39",MonoGroup & PaletNo,"")

	sol = D1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y8
	PartiNo = trim(S60T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo))
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(PartiNo)
	sol1 = Sol + 5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"LOT NUMARASI (H)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1.5, sol1 + 15,10,sag - sol1, PartiNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	if trim(PartiNo) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+6,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","H" & trim(PartiNo),"")
	End if
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(Y9 - 4, sag - 32,5,32, "ODETTE Ver.1 Rev.4")
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	PaletNo = S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)
	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("r345", "STOK60T", "BRUTAGIRLIK='"+PaletNo+"'", "", -1)
	for i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = S60T2.KOD(i)
		NetAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_4",StokKodu,"")
		PaletNetAgirligi = PaletNetAgirligi + NetAgirlik * S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Round(PaletNetAgirligi, 0)
End Function
Function GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	GetBrutAgirlik = NetAgirlik + KasaAgirligi
End Function
Function GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	PaletNo = S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)
	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("r345", "STOK60T", "BRUTAGIRLIK='"+PaletNo+"'", "", -1)
	S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
	for i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = S60T2.KOD(i)
		KasaAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",StokKodu,"")
		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KasaAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i), KasaIciMiktar))
		ToplamKasaAgirligi = ToplamKasaAgirligi + KasaAgirligi * KasaAdedi
	Next
	GetKasaAgirligi = Doc.Round(ToplamKasaAgirligi, 0)
End Function
Function GetKasaAdedi(satirNo)
	PaletNo = S60T.BRUTAGIRLIK(satirNo)
	Set S60T2 = Doc.Select2("r345", "STOK60T", "BRUTAGIRLIK='"+PaletNo+"'", "", -1)
	S60T2.GroupBy("KOD")
	for i = 1 to S60T2.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = S60T2.KOD(i)
		KasaIciMiktar = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_1",StokKodu,"")
		KasaAdedi = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(S60T2.SF_MIKTAR(i), KasaIciMiktar))
		ToplamKasaAdedi = ToplamKasaAdedi + KasaAdedi
	Next
	GetKasaAdedi = ToplamKasaAdedi
End Function

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, MainTableT
Sub PrintLabel1(TABLO)
	Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject(TABLO)
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 143
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 147.5
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(MainTableT.EVRAKNO(1)), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoChecksum(0)
	
	For i = 1 to MainTableT.GetRecCount()
		for k = 1 to 1 'her etiketten 2 tane
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
			if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
				Ust = UstMargin
				if EtiketNo <> 1 then
					res = pe1.PageBreak()
				end if
			else
				Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
			end if
			Call Etiket(pe1,i)
		next
	Next

	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
		Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	End if

	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	SeriNo = trim(MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo))

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dsdfwe", "D7KIDSLB", "SERINO='"+SeriNo+"'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub


	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 120)
	solSag = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(120,120,120))
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 25, solMargin, 80, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(solSag))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)


	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 95
	D2 = D1 + 36
	D3 = D1 + 48
	D4 = D1 + 72

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 20
	Y2 = Y1 + 13
	Y3 = Y2 + 13
	Y4 = Y3 + 30
	Y5 = Y4 + 10.5
	Y6 = Y4 + 26	'28
	Y7 = Y5 + 25  '28
	Y8 = Y6 + 20	'21
	Y9 = Y8 + 20  '23 

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, SolMargin, Y9 - Ust, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	solSag = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	if trim(solSag) = "SOL" Then
		res = pe1.PenSize(5)
		res = pe1.Line(Ust, SolMargin, Alt - Ust, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(Ust, solMargin, 0, 40)
		res = pe1.Line(Alt, solMargin, 0, 40)
	Elseif trim(solSag) = "SAÐ" or trim(solSag) = "SAG" Then
		res = pe1.PenSize(5)
		res = pe1.Line(Ust, SagMargin, Alt - Ust, 0)
		res = pe1.Line(Ust, sagMargin - 40, 0, 40)
		res = pe1.Line(Alt, sagMargin - 40, 0, 40)
	End if
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, SolMargin, 0, D1 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Y3 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D2, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D4, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, D1, Y9 - Y4, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D3, Y8 - Y7, 0)


	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
		Alici1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	Else
		CHKodu = d7LB.CH_MUSTERI

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_4",StokKodu,"") * MainTableT.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo)
	GetNetAgirlik = Doc.Round(NetAgirlik, 0)
End Function
Function GetBrutAgirlik(satirNo)
	NetAgirlik = GetNetAgirlik(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	GetBrutAgirlik = NetAgirlik + KasaAgirligi
End Function
Function GetKasaAgirligi(satirNo)
	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)
	KasaAgirligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_5",StokKodu,"")
	GetKasaAgirligi = Doc.Round(KasaAgirligi, 0)
End Function

Kod : 
Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, Adres, FirmaKodu, FirmaAdi, MainTableT
Sub PrintLabel1(TABLO)
	Set MainTableT = Doc.GetTableObject(TABLO)
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	FirmaAdi = "MAHLE FARPLAS"
	FirmaKodu = "00253892"

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	Ustmargin = 5
	EtiketBoyu = 148
	EtiketEni = 198
	EtiketlerArasi = 144
	solMargin = 6
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 6

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Set MainTableT = Doc.Select2("asda", "STOK60T", "FATURASIZMI<>'E'", "", -1)
	end if

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket" & trim(MainTableT.EVRAKNO(1)), sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(0)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoChecksum(0)

	
'	MainTableT.GroupBy("BRUTAGIRLIK")

	For i = 1 to MainTableT.GetRecCount()
		for k = 1 to 1 'her etiketten 2 tane
			EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if EtiketNo mod 2 = 1 then
				Ust = UstMargin
				if EtiketNo <> 1 then
					res = pe1.PageBreak()
				end if
			else
				Ust = UstMargin + EtiketlerArasi
			end if
			Call Etiket(pe1,i)
		next
	Next

	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Etiket(pe1,satirNo)
	'if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
	'	Set STOK60E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	'	Set STOK60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	'End if

	StokKodu = MainTableT.KOD(satirNo)

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 120)
	solSag = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "TEXT_TECH_2", StokKodu, "")
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(120,120,120))
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 25, solMargin, 80, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(solSag))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)


	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu
' dikey çizgilerin yerleri
	D1 = SolMargin + 95
	D2 = D1 + 36
	D3 = D1 + 48
	D4 = D1 + 72

'yatay çizgilerin yerleri
	Y1 = Ust + 17
	Y2 = Y1 + 12
	Y3 = Y2 + 12
	Y4 = Y3 + 30
	Y5 = Y4 + 10.5
	Y6 = Y4 + 28
	Y7 = Y5 + 28
	Y8 = Y6 + 21
	Y9 = Y8 + 23

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
'	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(200,200,200))
'	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, SolMargin, Y9 - Ust, sagMargin - SolMargin)
'	res = pe1.PenSize(3)
'	res = pe1.Line(Ust, SolMargin, Alt - Ust, 0)

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y5, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y6, SolMargin, 0, D1 - SolMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D1, 0, sagMargin - D1)
	res = pe1.Line(Y8, SolMargin, 0, sagMargin - SolMargin)

	res = pe1.Line(Ust, D1, Y3 - Ust, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D2, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, D4, Y3 - Y2, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y4, D1, Alt - Y4-5, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y7, D3, Y8 - Y7, 0)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Ust + 1

	SeriNo = trim(MainTableT.SERINO(satirNo))
	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dsdfwe", "D7KIDSLB", "SERINO='"+SeriNo+"'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub



	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Alici1 = STOK60E.SHIPTOCODE 			'LOC+11
		Alici2 = STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_1
		Alici3 = STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1
	Else
		Alici1 = "OYAK RENAULT"
	End if

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		CHKodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU
	Else
		CHKodu = d7LB.CH_MUSTERI
		Alici1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_2", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" )
		Alici2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")

	End if

	CHStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(MainTableT.KOD(satirNo) , CHKodu)
	Loc159= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0A", "NOTE_1", StokKodu&CHKodu , "EVRAKNO+CH_KODU" )


	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, SolMargin + 2.5 ,4,18,"ALICI")  'LOC+11
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",28)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, SolMargin + 12,18, sag - Sol+12, trim(Alici1))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",20)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 9, SolMargin + 2,10, sag - Sol,trim(Alici2))
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 7, SolMargin + 2,8, sag - Sol,trim(Alici3))

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Ust + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 1,4,50,"KAMYON ÝNDÝRME BÖLGESÝ") 		'LOC+159
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 48)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Loc159 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "ARTIKEL_REF_REF_3", STOK60T.SIPARTNO(satirNo),"ARTNO")
		'Loc159 = STOK60E.SHIPTO_UNVAN_2	
	End if

	res = pe1.Write(rUst - 1, sol, 30, sag - sol, trim(Loc159))

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = D1
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		IrsaliyeNo = trim(STOK60E.EXTERNALEVRAKNO)
	End if
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(IrsaliyeNo)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝRSALÝYE NO (N)")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol + 2,28,sag - sol,IrsaliyeNo)
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
'	res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+9,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","N" & trim(IrsaliyeNo) & "","")

	sol = D1 + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y1 + 1
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst, sol,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"ÝMALATÇI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 28)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, sol, 20, sag - sol, FirmaAdi)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5.5, sol, 15, sag - sol, Adres)

	sol = solMargin + 1
	sag = sagMargin
	rUst = Y3

	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(CHStokKodu)
	sol1 = Sol + 10
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",4)
	res = pe1.Write(rUst + 1.5, sol + 2.5,4,sag - sol - 2.5,"PARÇA NO (P)")
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",52)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(rUst - 2, sol1 + 10,25,sag - sol1 - 10,CHStokKodu)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		res = pe1.Barcode(rUst+16,sol1,13,BarkodGen,"CODE39","P" & CHStokKodu & "","")
	End if

	GuvenlikParcasi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_11", MainTableT.KOD(satirNo), "")
